#### [1. Open Source Flutter Apps](/content/tortuvshin/open-source-flutter-apps/README.md)

##### Productivity

*   [akou](https://openappscout.com/apps/akou/) - akou is a GPL-3.0 call recorder for Apple silicon Macs that transcribes on the device, with no bot in the meeting and no account, and saves the notes to files you own. ([Source (⭐14)](https://github.com/GeiserX/akou))

##### Tools

*   [VPN Bypass](https://openappscout.com/apps/vpn-bypass/) - A macOS menu bar app that sends chosen services and domains around a corporate VPN, or only those through it, and puts the routes back when the VPN reconnects. ([Source (⭐127)](https://github.com/GeiserX/VPN-Bypass))

##### Developer Tools

*   [Device Hub Pro](https://openappscout.com/apps/device-hub-pro/) - Native macOS app to manage and mirror Android emulators, Android phones, iOS simulators and iPhones side by side. ([Source (⭐40)](https://github.com/gitemre/device-hub-pro))
*   [Orbi](https://openappscout.com/apps/orbi/) - Orbi is an AGPL-3.0 system that turns labelled GitHub issues into reviewed, merged pull requests and tagged releases, using GitHub issues as its only state store; Orbi Cloud is the hosted offering. ([Source (⭐220)](https://github.com/orbi-build/orbi))
*   [SolidPing](https://openappscout.com/apps/solidping/) - SolidPing is an AGPL-3.0 uptime monitor that runs as a single Go binary or Docker image, on SQLite by default, with a hosted free plan. ([Source (⭐17)](https://github.com/fclairamb/solidping))

##### Games

*   [Patzer](https://openappscout.com/apps/patzer/) - Self-hosted Chess.com alternative: game review for your Chess.com and Lichess games, play vs Stockfish or a friend, AI coach on your own Ollama or vLLM, in one Docker container. ([Source (⭐42)](https://github.com/SikamikanikoBG/patzer))

##### Travel

*   [Pumperly](https://openappscout.com/apps/pumperly/) - Pumperly is an AGPL-3.0 map of fuel prices and EV chargers with a route planner, free to use at pumperly.com with no account or to run yourself with Docker Compose. ([Source (⭐44)](https://github.com/GeiserX/Pumperly))

#### [6. Awesome Parasite](/content/ecohealthalliance/awesome-parasite/README.md)

##### Scientific Journals

*   Bulletin de la Société de Pathologie Exotique - French journal on tropical medicine and public health. The journal ran from 1908 to 2020, when it was replaced by Médecine Tropicale et Santé Internationale. Full volumes are freely accessible from Université Paris Cité ([1908-1966, v. 1-59](https://www.biusante.parisdescartes.fr/histoire/medica/resultats/index.php?intro=bspe\&tri=\&p=1)), Gallica ([1967-1997, v. 60-90](https://gallica.bnf.fr/ark:/12148/cb344790278/date\&rk=21459;2)), The Society archives ([1994-2020, v. 87-113](https://pathexo.societe-mtsi.fr/1844-sommaire-bulletin_L=1.html)), and RevuesOnline ([2010-2020, v. 103-113](https://bspe.revuesonline.com/numeros_parus.jsp)).

##### Projects / AI Gateway

*   [agentgateway (⭐5.4k) (⭐5.4k)](https://github.com/agentgateway/agentgateway) - An open-source data plane for agentic AI connectivity, providing security, observability, and governance for agent-to-tool (MCP) and agent-to-agent (A2A) communication.

#### [19. Static Analysis](/content/analysis-tools-dev/static-analysis/README.md)

##### Programming Languages / [Other](#other-1)

*   **dephpend** :warning: — Dependency analysis tool.
*   [Prusti](https://www.pm.inf.ethz.ch/research/prusti.html) — A static verifier for Rust, based on the Viper verification infrastructure. By default Prusti verifies absence of panics by proving that statements such as unreachable!() and panic!() are unreachable.

##### Other / [Other](#other-1)

*   [htmlbeautifier (⭐373)](https://github.com/threedaymonk/htmlbeautifier) — A normaliser/beautifier for HTML that also understands embedded Ruby. Ideal for tidying up Rails templates.
*   [zanadir](https://mustachecase.github.io/) — Audits CI configuration (GitHub Actions, GitLab CI, CircleCI, Bitbucket Pipelines, Azure Pipelines, Jenkins) for missing security and quality controls such as SCA, secrets detection, SAST, IaC and workflow security, and grades whether each present control can actually fail the build (continue-on-error, || true, --exit-code 0, push-only triggers). Outputs table, JSON, SARIF or Markdown and prints CI snippets for the gaps.
*   **mdformat** :warning: — CommonMark compliant Markdown formatter

#### [20. Magictools](/content/ellisonleao/magictools/README.md)

##### Graphics / Bitmap Compression

*   :money\_with\_wings: [BatchSet](https://batchset.com/tools/image-compressor) - Compress and convert whole folders of PNG, JPG and WebP sprites in the browser, nothing uploaded.

##### Graphics / Spritesheet Tools

*   :free: [PicMason Sprite Sheet Splitter](https://picmason.com/en/sprite-sheet-splitter/) - Browser-based fixed-grid splitter with custom frame sizes, offsets and spacing. Exports PNG frames preserving source transparency and a ZIP with source coordinates; can skip fully transparent frames.

##### Graphics / Texture Tools

*   :tada: [MeshCoat](https://stuyk.github.io/meshcoat) - Fast, offline desktop texture painter: load a 3D model and paint PBR materials directly onto the mesh, then export texture maps. [Source (⭐12)](https://github.com/stuyk/meshcoat)
*   :tada: [Sprite Fusion Sprite Normals (⭐179)](https://github.com/Hugo-Dz/spritefusion-sprite-normals) - Generate normal maps from pixel art sprites and animations.

##### Graphics / Animation

*   :tada: [MeshAnimate](https://stuyk.github.io/meshanimate) - Pivot-style pose-to-pose animator for rigged Blender characters, with a simple timeline and onion-skin ghosts. Saves animations back into the .blend as normal Blender actions. [Source (⭐4)](https://github.com/stuyk/meshanimate)
*   :tada: [MeshRig](https://stuyk.github.io/meshrig) - Rig any mesh in a .blend in minutes: fit a starting skeleton, skin weights and IK. Writes a standard Blender armature that works anywhere a Blender rig does. [Source (⭐3)](https://github.com/stuyk/meshrig)

##### Graphics / Vector/Image Editor

*   :money\_with\_wings: [Pic2SVG](https://pic2svg.com/) - Raster-to-vector tracing preview for inspecting flat artwork intended for game icons or UI. Free anonymous split preview and enlarged view; downloads require Pro. Images are processed on the server.

##### Graphics / Modeling

*   :tada: [Inca](https://incacad.com) - Free, open-source 3D modeling, animation and rendering app that works like Maya 2020, with OBJ, FBX, glTF and STL import.

##### Engines and Frameworks / Voxel Editors

*   :tada: [Proof Engine (⭐31)](https://github.com/Mattbusel/proof-engine) - Rust rendering and game engine where every visual is math: glyphs and particles moved by ODEs, strange attractors and force fields, with OpenGL post-processing and synth audio.

##### AI / Voxel Editors

*   :tada: [Glitchless (⭐0)](https://github.com/glitchless-gg/glitchless) - Open-source game observability for Three.js, Phaser, Unity, Godot, Unreal and GameMaker: frame times with hitch causes, crashes, where players quit and player bug reports, plus an MCP server so coding agents can read the data and fix what players hit.
*   :free: [RespawnStack](https://respawnstack.com) - Free directory of pricing, commercial-use and ownership terms for 30+ AI game-dev tools. Each verified row cites its source (vendor page or Steamworks docs) with a link and date.
*   :free: [StarterLoot](https://starterloot.com) - Free directory of 80+ beginner friendly AI game dev tools, with guides like Godot vs Unity.
*   :free: [WishlistForge Steam Description Scorer](https://wishlistforge.com/tools/steam-description) - free in-browser scorer for Steam short descriptions. It checks length, hook, genre clarity, player fantasy and call to action, and suggests a rewrite. No account needed.

##### Collections / Voxel Editors

*   :free: [DailySounds](https://dailysounds.org/?ref=magictools) - Searchable sound effects for games, with browser previews and downloadable audio; free for commercial projects under the DailySounds license.
*   :free: [PulseLite UI SFX](https://bigshadex.itch.io/pulselite-ui-sfx-free) - 20 free UI sounds (clicks, hovers, confirms, errors, rewards) in WAV/OGG, procedurally synthesized with no samples or AI audio models, sounds CC BY 4.0, plus an optional Godot 4 drop-in script (MIT).

##### Music and Audio Editors / Voxel Editors

*   :money\_with\_wings: [CICA AUDIO](https://cicaaudio.com/) - AI sound effect generator for games and UI. Analyzes gameplay recordings, detects sound events, and generates synchronized WAV files.
*   :tada: [Slip Sound](https://stuyk.github.io/slip-sound) - Fast, local desktop audio sample manager for searching and organizing large sound libraries. No accounts or file caps, with MCP support. [Source (⭐12)](https://github.com/stuyk/slip-sound)

##### Project Management / Voxel Editors

*   :money\_with\_wings: [Gamifying](https://gamifying.dev/) - Browser workspace for game design: characters, quests, items and lore as linked entries, with Kanban board, timeline and JSON export.

#### [23. Awesome Testing](/content/TheJambo/awesome-testing/README.md)

##### Software / UI & End-to-End Testing

*   [Device Hub Pro (⭐40)](https://github.com/gitemre/device-hub-pro) - Free, open-source macOS app for manual testing on Android emulators, Android phones and iOS simulators side by side, with live mirroring, device controls (network, location, permissions, deep links, push), logs, screenshots and screen recordings.
*   [App Crash Lab (⭐3)](https://github.com/aichance/business-ai-recipes/tree/main/tools/app-crash-lab) - MIT-licensed Node 22+/Playwright CLI that checks saved web-app values after reload and rejected updates, exporting state diffs and standalone Playwright tests.
*   [Browser Use (⭐119k)](https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use) - Open-source (MIT) Python AI agent that runs plain-English tasks in a real browser. Browser Use Cloud adds hosted stealth Chromium you can drive over CDP from Playwright, Puppeteer or Selenium, with session recordings and live view.
*   [ContextQA MCP](https://mcp.contextqa.com/docs) - Hosted MCP server for creating and running end-to-end tests and investigating failures, with OAuth browser sign-in.
*   [Proxy QA Browser (⭐1)](https://github.com/UbaidBinWaris/proxy_browser) - Free, open-source desktop app to test your own web forms by US location, emulated device and browser, with recorded scenarios, consent and accessibility checks, and a CI runner.
*   [vibe-testing (⭐17)](https://github.com/AishwaryShrivastav/vibe-testing) - Open-source MCP server and CLI that scans web app routes and forms, runs browser checks with Playwright, and produces HTML QA reports.
*   [Device Changer](https://deviceschanger.org) - Applies device models, build fingerprints and identifiers to rooted Android test devices to reproduce device-specific bugs.

##### Software / Accessibility & Usability Testing

*   [Oh My Android (⭐60)](https://github.com/ateymoori/oh-my-android) - Free, open-source macOS app for checking Android apps on emulators and phones in dark mode, large font sizes, RTL and TalkBack, with a TalkBack-order accessibility audit.
*   [AuditReady](https://feaefe371424d0725b266468856c9101.ctonew.app/) - Runs 12 deterministic HTML checks on a page against WCAG 2.2 and returns the matching criterion and a suggested fix for each finding, then turns them into a printable report an agency can hand to its client. Free instant scan; the full report is a one-off $29.
*   [Site Spell Checker](https://sitespellchecker.com) - Crawls a live website and flags spelling and grammar mistakes on every page, each shown in context with its page URL. Free one-page scan; the full-site scan is a one-off $99.

##### Software / API Testing

*   [API Spector](https://api-spector.dev) - Open-source API clien, mock server and contract testing tool. Zero telemetry.
*   [Openhook (⭐1)](https://github.com/openhook-dev/openhook-inbox) - Open-source webhook inbox for capturing callbacks and inspecting request headers and bodies through a browser, REST API, or MCP.
*   [SolidPing (⭐17)](https://github.com/fclairamb/solidping) - Open-source (AGPL-3.0) uptime and API monitoring with a free hosted tier. Runs HTTP, gRPC, WebSocket and custom JS checks on a schedule and alerts when an endpoint fails.
*   [BestJSON JSON Diff](https://bestjson.com/json-diff) - Free browser tool for comparing saved JSON API responses by structure, with explicit array-order and record-ID matching rules.

##### Software / AI & LLM Testing

*   [fanout-check (⭐1)](https://github.com/sunxiuguo/fanout-check) - Offline Python library and CLI that checks observed concurrency, overlap, and launch timing in explicitly grouped tool or agent calls.

##### Software / Service Virtualization

*   [MockForge (⭐12)](https://github.com/SaaSy-Solutions/mockforge) - Open-source API mock server written in Rust for HTTP/OpenAPI, gRPC, GraphQL, WebSocket, Kafka, MQTT and AMQP, with data generation and latency/fault injection.
*   [fieldtwin](https://nickgeorgeai.com/fieldtwin) - Open-source, local, stateful twins of the Jobber and QuickBooks Online APIs for integration and AI agent tests, with checkpoints, rewind, and faults on demand.
*   [OutageFence (⭐0)](https://github.com/jn6825rsr7-afk/outagefence) - Local HTTP fault-injection CLI by Slanta Cause that checks synthetic sensitive data against declared destination rules, with positive controls and offline reports.

##### Software / Visual Testing

*   [CurlShot](https://curlshot.com) - Screenshot API for capturing baseline and comparison images of web pages in CI as PNG, JPEG, WebP or PDF, with full-page and CSS-selector element capture, device presets and ad/cookie-banner blocking. Free plan available.
*   [cloneproof (⭐0)](https://github.com/pcjansen09-max/cloneproof) - Scores how closely a website clone matches the original (pixels, layout, style, scroll motion) with Playwright and lists the exact differences.

##### Software / Test Report Diagnostics

*   [junit-collision-check (⭐0)](https://github.com/sunxiuguo/junit-collision-check) - Offline Python CLI that detects duplicate test identities and conflicting outcomes across JUnit XML reports before CI report ingestion.

##### Software / Test Data Management

*   [TempMail Hub](https://tempmailhub.net/) - Free disposable inbox for manual or scripted signup and email-verification testing, with a selectable lifetime from 10 minutes to 7 days and no account required.
*   [Mails.ai](https://mails.ai/docs/email-testing) - Email inboxes for automated tests: a plus address per test and one call that waits for the email and returns the sign-up code or magic link, with TypeScript and Python SDKs, Cypress and Playwright packages and an MCP server.
*   [ByteRivet Sample Files](https://byterivet.com/test-files/) - Free browser-based generator for exact-size dummy files from 1 KB to 2 GB in 22 formats (PDF, PNG, JPG, DOCX, XLSX, CSV, JSON, ZIP, MP4, WAV and more) for upload-limit and file-handling tests. Files are built locally, no upload or signup.
*   [FormFiller test data tools](https://formfillerapp.com/tools/) - Free browser-based generators for fake addresses, identities, IBANs, test card numbers and bulk CSV/JSON/SQL data in 22
    locales.

##### Software / Browser Extensions & Utilities

*   [Keploy API Test Recorder](https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/keploy-api-test-recorder/ohcclfkaidblnjnggclkiecgkpgldihe) - Chrome extension that records API calls from your browser session and turns them into API tests.
*   [Spoof Me](https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/spoof-me/onbjkmhileedchimkcmieeiomipdljki) - Test how a site behaves for users in other browsers, OSes, languages, timezones and screen sizes by switching the whole browser identity. Also available for Firefox.

##### Software / Performance & Load Testing

*   [check-proxy-tax (⭐0)](https://github.com/600me/check-proxy-tax) - Zero-dependency CLI that samples a URL concurrently, then reports the 403 retry tax: usable-response rate and effective cost per 1,000 usable responses instead of the nominal per-1,000 price.

##### Software / Test Automation Frameworks

*   [behave (⭐2)](https://github.com/emirmrkaljevic/behave) - Records how a command-line tool behaves (flags, stdin, exit codes, edge cases) as golden tests and replays them against another implementation, e.g. a Rust or Go rewrite.
*   [VeriSpec (⭐0)](https://github.com/godikemahesh/verispec) - Spec-driven quality engineering framework that turns specifications into traceable test strategies, native test suites, and continuous release evidence.

##### Software / Static Analysis

*   [SmartBench (⭐2)](https://github.com/xianyu-sheng/SmartBench) - Open-source CLI for evidence-constrained code audits: deterministic static analysis owns the facts and LLM hypotheses must be validated against the code before they become findings.

##### Newsletters / Static Analysis

*   [RoleGig QA & Testing](https://rolegig.com/qa-testing/) - Weekly list of QA, test automation and SDET jobs in India, the US, Canada, Europe, Australia and the Gulf.

#### [33. Awesome Machine Learning](/content/josephmisiti/awesome-machine-learning/README.md)

##### Python / General-Purpose Machine Learning

#### [47. Awesome Amazon Seller](/content/ScaleLeap/awesome-amazon-seller/README.md)

##### Software and Tools

*   [ChineseCheck](https://chinesecheck.com) - Verify a Chinese supplier before paying: look up the company by English name, Chinese name or Unified Social Credit Code and read its registration record in English for free; paid reports add court enforcement, litigation and penalty records.
*   [ScrapeUnblocker](https://docs.scrapeunblocker.com/plugins/amazon) - Web scraping API with Amazon endpoints that return JSON: product pages (price, list price, availability, rating, seller, images), search results with sponsored and Prime flags, Best Sellers and other ranked lists, and review data with the star histogram, across Amazon's regional marketplaces. Also available as an MCP server.
*   [RefVid](https://refvid.com) - AI product video generator that keeps label, logo and packaging consistent across clips, from 1-5 photos or an Amazon product link. Free credits on sign-up.
*   [Utlista](https://www.utlista.com) - Localises an existing product catalogue into Amazon listings for seven EU marketplaces (de, fr, it, es, nl, pl, se). Writes title, five bullets, description and backend search terms per destination marketplace rather than translating them, and validates every field against Amazon's limits before export, including search terms counted in bytes. Produces a flat file per marketplace; it does not upload to Amazon. Free for ten products.
*   [Vendooly](https://vendooly.com/en/) - MCP connector that brings Seller Central and Amazon Ads into ChatGPT and Claude through the official Amazon APIs. The AI prepares each change with a before/after preview and it runs only after your approval. 14-day free trial.
*   [VNCNT Labs Keyword Research](https://apify.com/vncntlabs/keyword-ideas) - Reverse ASIN (the Amazon keywords any ASIN ranks for) and keyword ideas from a seed via Amazon related searches and Google autocomplete, with search volume, CPC, difficulty and a USPTO trademark flag. Runs on Apify, pay per result from $0.002 per keyword, no subscription.

#### [48. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### MCP Servers

*   [BlockVectra (⭐0)](https://github.com/blockvectra/mcp) - MCP server for Robinhood Chain and multiple EVM networks. Connect over remote Streamable HTTP to discover chains and read docs without a key; RPC access follows each chain's policy, while Data API and account tools need an API key. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/blockvectra/mcp --ref main`.
*   [Firecrawl](https://docs.firecrawl.dev/quickstarts/gemini-cli?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=integration) - Official Firecrawl MCP server for web search, page scraping to markdown or JSON, and site crawling. The hosted server works keyless (rate limited) with search, scrape and parse; add an [API key](https://docs.firecrawl.dev/mcp-server/keyless?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=integration#add-an-api-key) for crawl, map and the rest. The open-source server is MIT ([source (⭐7.7k)](https://github.com/firecrawl/firecrawl-mcp-server)). Add with: `gemini mcp add --transport http firecrawl https://mcp.firecrawl.dev/v2/mcp`.
*   [npmscan (⭐1)](https://github.com/salemalem/npmscan-mcp-plugin) - npm supply-chain security: checks packages for malware, known vulnerabilities, typosquats, maintainer takeovers and risky install scripts before install, and audits package.json, lockfiles, SBOMs and PR dependency changes. 23 read-only tools, no API key, no signup. Remote Streamable HTTP, bundled with 5 skills as a Gemini CLI extension. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/salemalem/npmscan-mcp-plugin`.
*   [deed.page](https://deed.page/docs?ref=skill-awesome-gemini-cli) - Static hosting for agents with a remote MCP server that lets Gemini CLI publish an HTML file or static folder to a permanent public `https://<slug>.deed.page` URL and update it with the token it gets back. No account or API key for publishing; the companion Agent Skill is MIT ([source (⭐1)](https://github.com/f-o-x11/deed-page-skill)). Remote Streamable HTTP. Add with `gemini mcp add --transport http deed-page https://deed.page/mcp`.
*   [ppl](https://withppl.com/agents?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Personal CRM for humans with AI agents, with a hosted remote MCP server (OAuth) at `https://withppl.com/mcp`: Gemini CLI can look up contacts, log interactions, keep notes and set reminders about the people in your life. Free beta up to 1,000 contacts at <https://withppl.com/register>.
*   [Super (⭐3)](https://github.com/fitzyracing1/Super) - Permission-gated superuser (sysadmin) MCP server that lets Gemini CLI check system status, disks and processes, run shell commands and read GitHub, behind a dangerous-command denylist, human approval gates, optional Cisco AI Defense and Duo authorization, and an append-only audit log. Python, local stdio; `pip install superuser`, then add the `superuser` command to your Gemini CLI MCP server config.
*   [Cooper Email](https://cooperemail.com/docs/mcp?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Email inboxes for agents with a hosted Streamable HTTP MCP server at `https://cooperemail.com/mcp` (OAuth or API key): Gemini CLI gets its own `username@cooperemail.com` address to send, read, and reply to mail and wait for replies or sign-up codes. The free plan includes 5 inboxes.
*   [CodeTrendy](https://codetrendy.com/mcp?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Search CodeTrendy, a curated directory of newly launched startups, SaaS tools and indie products, from Gemini CLI: keyword search with category and pricing filters, full listing profiles, alternatives to a named product, trending listings and the site of the day. 7 read-only tools. Remote Streamable HTTP, no API key, no account: `gemini mcp add --transport http codetrendy https://codetrendy.com/api/mcp`.
*   [MEDIAPRONET](https://mediapronet.com/mcp?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Search the MEDIAPRONET launch and discovery directory from Gemini CLI: keyword search over newly launched startups, SaaS tools and online products (category and pricing filters), full listing profiles, alternatives to a named product, categories and what is trending. 7 read-only tools. Remote Streamable HTTP, no API key, no account. Works with any MCP client including Gemini CLI: `gemini mcp add --transport http mediapronet https://mediapronet.com/api/mcp`.
*   [LLMSlim (⭐21)](https://github.com/Thanatos9404/llmslim) - Long-term memory for Gemini CLI and other MCP clients over a local stdio MCP server. Five tools (`remember`, `recall`, `list_memories`, `forget`, `fit_context`): messages are stored word for word in local SQLite or your own Postgres, and `recall` returns the stored messages that answer a question within a token budget, with no LLM call inside. MIT. Install: `pip install "llmslim[mcp]"`, then `gemini mcp add --scope user llmslim llmslim mcp`.
*   [Aria Icons (⭐101)](https://github.com/LeulAria/Aria-Icons) - MCP server with 12 tools for searching \~1.4M open-source SVG icons and adding them as SVG or React/Vue/Svelte/Solid components; remote endpoint needs no auth. Add with `gemini mcp add --transport http aria-icons https://icons.leularia.com/api/mcp`.
*   [SitePatent](https://sitepatent.com/mcp?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Search SitePatent, a screened discovery platform for newly launched startups, tools and online products, from Gemini CLI: keyword search with category and pricing filters, full listing profiles, alternatives to a named product, categories and trending listings. 7 read-only tools. Remote Streamable HTTP, no API key, no account: `gemini mcp add --transport http sitepatent https://sitepatent.com/api/mcp`.

##### Interfaces

*   [Alchemist Coder (⭐5)](https://github.com/Alekla0126/alchemist-coder) - Open-source desktop app for Windows, macOS and Linux that indexes your Gemini CLI chats, together with Claude Code, Codex and Grok Build sessions, into one local, read-only full-text search. Running Gemini CLI over ACP inside the app is planned for its upcoming Pro edition; the free edition runs Claude Code and Codex on local models.
*   [SwarmPane](https://swarmpane.com/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Desktop app for Mac, Windows and Linux that runs Gemini CLI (with a Gemini API key) side by side with Claude Code, Codex, Cursor Agent, Copilot, Devin and more, up to 16 at once in real terminals, with a git worktree per agent, undo for every agent step and account switching when a usage limit hits. Paid, with a 7-day trial for $1.

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

*   [AutoHarness (⭐2)](https://github.com/D-Shey/autoharness) - Makes Gemini CLI (and Claude Code, Codex, Qwen Code) prepare before it builds: splits the task into areas of know-how, finds or writes a skill for each, checks the result against the original, and lets a fresh critic judge it. MIT.
*   [Concord MCP (⭐602)](https://github.com/Get-Concord-AI/concord-mcp) - Local-first shared work-state MCP server (`npx -y @concord-ai/concord-mcp`) that lets coding agents coordinate on one repo through presence, file claims, messaging, handoffs and review packets. Works with Gemini CLI alongside Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and other agents. TypeScript, stdio, MIT.
*   [LitGravity (⭐166)](https://github.com/wjgoarxiv/litgravity) - Adds lit to the Antigravity CLI (`agy`) so a request is planned, built with tests and recorded checks, reviewed, and closed with a handoff note.

##### Commands & Extensions

*   [US Watchlist Alerts (⭐2)](https://github.com/eli-alvarado/us-watchlist-alerts) - Agent Skills (SKILL.md) for a US stock watchlist: NYSE-aware, de-duplicated price alerts (levels, % moves, gaps, 52-week highs/lows, MA crosses, RSI, earnings) and morning/close briefs. Stdlib Python, no API key, never places trades. MIT. Install with `npx skills add eli-alvarado/us-watchlist-alerts -y -a gemini-cli`.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

*   [ChatVault (⭐26)](https://github.com/ssanaullahrais/Chat-Vault) - Windows app that reads, searches and backs up your Gemini CLI chats across accounts, and continues any chat in Gemini CLI through a handoff file that carries the whole conversation.
*   [laya-guard (⭐30)](https://github.com/scs0209/laya-guard) - Local guardrail with a `BeforeTool` hook for Gemini CLI (`laya-guard install gemini-cli`) that judges each shell command before it runs, using regex rules, protected paths, and a small on-device model. Blocked commands are denied; ones marked for review pass with a note. Also hooks into Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and other agents. MIT.
*   [ratchet (⭐3)](https://github.com/krishansubudhi/ratchet) - Code-size budget CLI (`pip install ratchet-size`): `ratchet check` refuses changes that grow source or test line counts past a ceiling, and only `ratchet grant` raises it. For Gemini CLI it is wired through a `GEMINI.md` instruction snippet (no hook); a CI check or git pre-commit hook enforces it.
*   [OpenMoat (⭐2)](https://github.com/crocodile-labs/openmoat) - Local policy layer with a `BeforeTool` hook for Gemini CLI (`openmoat init`) that checks every shell command, file read and write, web request and MCP call against one policy before it runs, blocking what the policy denies. Adds an egress proxy, MCP server pinning and a hash-chained audit log, and the same policy covers Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and other agents. Rust, MIT OR Apache-2.0.

##### Documentation & Examples

*   [MCP servers, harness by harness (RigFit)](https://rigfit-harness.vercel.app/mcp-servers-by-harness/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - One MCP server (Context7) configured in Gemini CLI's settings.json alongside Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode and goose, using each tool's documented syntax, with sources dated.

##### AI Tools / Other Tools

[![Open-Source Software](https://jaywcjlove.github.io/sb/ico/min-oss.svg "Open Source Software")](https://github.com/benord-labs/frink) ![Freeware](https://jaywcjlove.github.io/sb/ico/min-free.svg "Freeware")

#### [54. Awesome Webgl](/content/sjfricke/awesome-webgl/README.md)

##### Articles

*   [Houdini to Three.js: Testing glTF Compression and Instancing](https://digitalgarden.si/blog/houdini-gltf-threejs-optimization/) - Case study of geometry, texture compression and instancing with WebGPU and WebGL2 checks.

##### Tools/Debugging

*   [Fast3D](https://www.fast3d.org/en) - Browser-based 3D toolbox: convert between GLB, GLTF, OBJ, STL, FBX and PLY, compress models and preview them in a full-featured viewer. Everything runs client-side.
*   [Web3D Viewer (⭐2)](https://github.com/userzz1145141919810-rgb/web-3d-viewer) - Single-file OBJ viewer that logs how every MTL texture reference was resolved (exact path, extension-stripped, fuzzy) and which ones failed, so missing materials are traceable instead of silently rendering black.

##### Tutorials / Firefox Specific Tools/Debugger

*   [TutorialSearch](https://tutorialsearch.io/browse/web-development/webgl-graphics) - Free cross-platform search engine indexing 50,000+ tutorials from Udemy, Skillshare, Pluralsight, and other major learning platforms across 45+ categories.
*   [A tutorial on infinite canvas with WebGL](https://infinitecanvas.cc) - A hands-on tutorial for building a GPU-accelerated infinite canvas in the browser, with first-class WebGL support (and WebGPU) via a unified graphics API—covering ECS, SDF-based 2D primitives, GPU text, and tile-based rendering, by [@xiaoiver](https://github.com/xiaoiver).

##### Articles / Firefox Specific Tools/Debugger

*   [Zero Bytes of Geometry: A Metal Lattice Sphere-Traced in a 14.9 KB Page](https://dev.to/smirnovartur/zero-bytes-of-geometry-a-metal-lattice-sphere-traced-in-a-149-kb-page-kib) - Ray marching a signed distance field in raw WebGL 2, with an analytic environment in place of an HDR file and resolution adapted against the display refresh period.

##### Compute (GPGPU) / Computer Vision

*   [Speedy Vision (⭐193)](https://github.com/alemart/speedy-vision) - GPU-accelerated Computer Vision library for JavaScript.

##### Compute (GPGPU) / Particles

*   [NixieFX (⭐19)](https://github.com/azakhary/nixie-fx) - Particle and VFX runtime for PixiJS and Three.js, authored in a browser-based editor.

##### Maps and Visualizations / Particles

*   [Globedots (⭐2)](https://github.com/swamoth/globedots) - Dot-matrix globe on WebGL2 in 17 kB, with markers, arcs, labels, heat maps, and a day-night line.
*   [leaflet-webgl-markers (⭐3)](https://github.com/tang-tc/leaflet-webgl-markers) - Leaflet plugin for rendering millions of point markers on a single WebGL canvas, with FBO picking and zero redraw while dragging.
*   [TradeCanvas (⭐28)](https://github.com/bonguynvan/tradecanvas) - Financial charting library that draws candles, indicators and depth heatmaps with WebGL 2, falling back to Canvas 2D.
*   [WorldSeed (⭐1)](https://github.com/yz4git/next-gen) - Turns open geospatial data into playable, exportable Three.js worlds with terrain-aware roads, GLB, road graphs, spawn points, and versioned JSON Schema.

##### WebAR / Particles

*   [encantar.js (⭐125)](https://github.com/alemart/encantar-js) - GPU-accelerated Augmented Reality library that works on any device (iOS, Android, desktops). Easy to use and requires no WebXR support.

##### WebGL 2 / Particles

*   [dither-flow (⭐1)](https://github.com/caleblemos/dither-flow) - Cursor-stirred 1-bit dithered flow field, WebGL 2 only, zero dependencies.
*   [textmode.js (⭐174)](https://github.com/humanbydefinition/textmode.js) - Lightweight, framework-agnostic creative coding library for real-time ASCII and textmode graphics using WebGL2.
*   [Vysmo Transitions (⭐9)](https://github.com/vysmodev/vysmo) - Tree-shakable WebGL 2 library of 60 endpoint-correct transitions with mesh and multi-pass support, \~5 KB gzipped.

##### Others / Particles

*   [img2threejs (⭐19k)](https://github.com/hoainho/img2threejs) - Toolkit that rebuilds the object in a reference image as a code-only, procedural Three.js model - primitives, procedural shaders, generated geometry, no downloaded meshes.
*   [zephyr3d](https://zephyr3d.org/) - A TypeScript-based 3D rendering engine with WebGPU/WebGL support.

#### [55. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### APIs, Data, and ML

*   [cors.dev](https://cors.dev/) - CORS proxy for calling third-party APIs from frontend code: prefix any HTTPS URL with `https://proxy.cors.dev/`. Free without signup or an API key for GET and HEAD requests, 600 requests per minute per IP, responses up to 1 MiB, no monthly quota.
*   [Dynamic Document API](https://dynamicdocumentapi.com/) - Turn data into pixel-perfect PDFs and images. The free plan includes 50 renders per month and access to 2 templates from the template gallery or your own.

##### Tools for Teams and Collaboration

*   [Enchant](https://www.enchant.com) - Omnichannel customer communication platform. Free tier includes chat widgets, email newsletters, link-in-bio hub & more.
*   [MergeMe](https://mergeme.dev/) - MergeMe brings GitHub/GitLab PRs/MRs directly into Slack as 1 constantly updating card. It adds CI status, mirrored comments, @mentions, reminders & more. Free for teams up to 5 developers & 1 channel mapping.

##### Monitoring

*   [OpenUptime](https://openuptime.app) - Hosted uptime monitoring and status pages. Free tier includes 3 active monitors with 5-minute check intervals, 26,000 checks and 100 emails per month, and 30 days of check history.

##### Education and Career Development

*   [BylineCV](https://bylinecv.com/) - Form-based CV and cover letter builder to pass ATS filters and organize all your versions in one place. Free forever for core CV building, exports, and storage, with daily AI limits.

##### Email

*   [DMARCmetric](https://dmarcmetric.com/) - DMARC report monitoring with named sending sources, SPF/DKIM alignment and plain-English summaries. Free for 1 domain: 10,000 messages/month, 30-day history, email alerts, no credit card.
*   [SignupVet](https://www.signupvet.com) - An API that checks email, phone and IP of new signups. 250 free checks per month, no credit card.

##### Forms

*   [BootForm](https://bootform.com/) - Form backend for builders. Free plan: 250 submissions a month with unlimited forms. Email and Discord notifications, honeypot/CAPTCHA, and an MCP server so AI coding agents can create and configure forms and manage submissions.

##### Web Hosting

*   [Pagehaus](https://pagehaus.app) - Hosting for plain HTML sites, uploaded over SFTP or in the browser. Free plan: 1 site, 250 MB storage, 20 GB transfer/month, HTTPS on a pagehaus.site subdomain, contact forms, Server Side Includes and cookie-free analytics. No expiry, no card.

##### Data Visualization on Maps

*   [opengeocoding.com](https://opengeocoding.com) - Source-available geocoder in Rust: address search, autocomplete and reverse geocoding on open data. Self-host it or use the hosted API. Fifty thousand free queries/month.
*   [waymorphic.com](https://waymorphic.com/) - Vector map tiles (OpenMapTiles schema), MapLibre styles, geocoding and reverse geocoding APIs. No API key, no request limit, fair use policy.

#### [57. Awesome Zig](/content/zigcc/awesome-zig/README.md)

##### Fundamentals / Documentation and Testing

*   [newmathphys/kern (⭐8)](https://github.com/newmathphys/kern) - Zero-trust verification gateway: a claim is admitted only with machine evidence, so every Z3 proof DAG is re-checked inside KERN's own Zig kernel and each counterexample is replayed against the real function in Python (for C/Zig/Rust the counterexample comes from the SMT model with machine-type bounds). Contract annotations (`@kern:requires`/`@kern:ensures`) are checked in Python, Zig, C and Rust, and the toolchain ships 125 Lean 4 theorems with 0 `sorry`. AI-assisted.

##### Fundamentals / Performance Benchmark

*   [jnordwick/zmida (⭐12)](https://github.com/jnordwick/zmida) - Benchmarking harness for CPU-bound code with adaptive trials, TSC timing, perf counters and text, CSV or gnuplot output. AI-assisted.

##### Language Essentials / File Format Processing

*   [JacobCrabill/zigdown (⭐138)](https://github.com/JacobCrabill/zigdown) - A Markdown toolset, not CommonMark-compliant by design, that renders to the terminal, HTML and Neovim, formats files and runs in-terminal slide shows. AI-assisted.
*   [guanchzhou/zig-pkl (⭐0)](https://github.com/guanchzhou/zig-pkl) - Bindings for Apple Pkl over the Message Passing API, with typed evaluation, custom and external readers, and a Pkl-to-Zig code generator. AI-assisted.

##### Systems Programming / Operating Systems

*   [NoamRothschild/hypervisor (⭐13)](https://github.com/NoamRothschild/hypervisor) - A type 1 hypervisor for Intel VT-x, written from scratch in Zig, that boots an unmodified Linux kernel to a userspace shell. AI-assisted.

##### Network & Web / Network

*   [guanchzhou/zig-telegram (⭐0)](https://github.com/guanchzhou/zig-telegram) - Telegram Bot API 10.3 client, pure-Zig MTProto 2.0 core, and TDLib bridge for Zig 0.17. AI-assisted.

##### Network & Web / Web Framework

*   [ziex-dev/ziex (⭐398)](https://github.com/ziex-dev/ziex) - A full-stack web framework for Zig. [ziex.dev](https://ziex.dev).

##### Network & Web / Web3 Framework

*   [okcontract/oksolc (⭐58)](https://github.com/okcontract/oksolc) - A Solidity compiler written in Zig that aims for byte-compatible output with solc 0.8.36 on its supported path, optimized via-IR compilation through Standard JSON, intended for development rather than production deployment. AI-assisted.
*   [omerfirmak/zevm (⭐21)](https://github.com/omerfirmak/zevm) - An alpha Ethereum Virtual Machine in Zig that dispatches opcodes with threaded code, usable as a library with a pluggable state backend. AI-assisted.

##### Data & Science / Database

*   [antflydb/antfly (⭐470)](https://github.com/antflydb/antfly) - A search-and-inference database written in Zig, with full-text, vector and graph indexes over the same table. AI-assisted.

##### Data & Science / Encryption

*   [myzonerocks/gossveil (⭐1)](https://github.com/myzonerocks/gossveil) - End-to-end encryption library for messaging applications, written in Zig. No-AI.

## Contribution

This repo is generated by [trackawesomelist-source](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source), if you want to add your awesome list here, please edit [config.yml](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source/blob/main/config.yml), and send a pull request, or just open an [issue](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source/issues), I'll add it manually.

If you want to add badge ([![Track Awesome List](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/badge.svg)](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/ripienaar/free-for-dev/)
) to your awesome list, please add the following code to your README.md:

```markdown
[![Track Awesome List](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/badge.svg)](https://www.trackawesomelist.com/your_repo_pathname/)
```


The doc is still under construction, if you have any question, please open an [issue](https://github.com/trackawesomelist/trackawesomelist-source/issues)
